```java
public class DivArrayByTwo {
    //@  requires arr == null;
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@ also
    //@  requires arr != null;
    //@  assigns arr[*];
    //@  ensures \forall int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; arr[i] == \old(arr[i]) / 2;
    //@ behaviors complete;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    public static void DivArrayByTwo(int[] arr) {
        if (arr == null)
            return;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= arr.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; arr[j] == \old(arr[j]) / 2;
        //@ maintaining \forall int k; i <= k < arr.length; arr[k] == \old(arr[k]);
        //@ loop_writes arr[*], i;
        //@ decreases arr.length - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length; ++i) {
            arr[i] = arr[i] / 2;
        }
    }
}
```

user: ```java
public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

```
assistant: ```java
public class IsPrefix {
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() <= txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == (\forall int i; 0 <= i < pat.length(); pat.charAt(i) == txt.charAt(i));
    //@ also
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() > txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == false;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= pat.length();
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; pat.charAt(j) == txt.charAt(j);
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases pat.length() - i;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}
```

```java
public class Find {
    //@  requires arr != null;
    //@  requires \exists int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; arr[i] == elem;
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures 0 <= \result < arr.length;
    //@  ensures \forall int i; 0 <= i < \result; arr[i] != elem;
    //@  ensures arr[\result] == elem;
    //@ also
    //@  requires arr == null || \forall int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; arr[i] != elem;
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == -1;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    //@ behaviors complete;
    //@ pure
    public static int find(int[] arr, int elem) {
        if (arr == null)
            return -1;
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= arr.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; arr[j] != elem;
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases arr.length - i;

        return -1;
```

Please generate JML specifications for the Java program given below.
```java
public class IdxFirstElemWithDuplicate {
    public static int idxFirstElemWithDuplicate(int[] arr) {
        if (arr == null || arr.length == 0) return -1;

        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length - 1; i++) {
            for (int j = i + 1; j < arr.length; j++) {
                if (arr[i] == arr[j]) {
                    return i;
                }
            }
        }
        return -1;
    }
}

```

```java
public class IdxFirstElemWithDuplicate {
```
```java
    /*@ public normal_behavior
      @ requires arr != null && arr.length > 0;
      @ assigns \nothing;
```
      @ ensures (\result == -1 && (\exists int i, j; 0 <= i < arr.length && 0 - 1 <= j <= arr.length || i != j; arr[i] != arr[j]))   
      @         || (0 <= \result < arr.length && (\exists int j; \result < j < arr.length; arr[\result] == arr[j]));
      @*/
    public static int idxFirstElemWithDuplicate(int[] arr) {
        if (arr == null || arr.length == 0) return -1;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= arr.length - 1;
        //@ maintaining (\forall int k, l; 0 <= k < i && i < l < arr.length; arr[k] != arr[l]);
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases arr.length - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length - 1; i++) {
            //@ maintaining i + 1 <= j <= arr.length;
            //@ maintaining (\forall int k; i < k < j; arr[i] != arr[k]);  
            //@ loop_writes j;
            //@ decreases arr.length - j;
            for (int j = i + 1; j < arr.length; j++) {
                if (arr[i] == arr[j]) {
                    return i;
                }
            }
        }
        return -1;
    }
}

public class IdxFirstElemWithDuplicate {
    /*@ public normal_behavior
      @ requires arr != null && arr.length > 0;
      @ assigns \nothing;
      @ ensures (\result == -1 && (\forall int i, j; 0 - 1 <= i < arr.length && 0 - 1 <= j <= arr.length || i != j; arr[i] != arr[j]))   
      @         || (0 <= \result < arr.length && (\exists int j; \result < j < arr.length; arr[\result] == arr[j]));
      @*/
    public static int idxFirstElemWithDuplicate(int[] arr) {
        if (arr == null || arr.length == 0) return -1;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= arr.length - 1;
        //@ maintaining (\forall int k, l; 0 <= k < i && i < l < arr.length; arr[k] != arr[l]);
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases arr.length - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length - 1; i++) {
            //@ maintaining i + 1 <= j <= arr.length;
            //@ maintaining (\forall int k; i < k < j; arr[i] != arr[k]);  
            //@ loop_writes j;
            //@ decreases arr.length - j;
            for (int j = i + 1; j < arr.length; j++) {
                if (arr[i] == arr[j]) {
                    return i;
                }
            }
        }
        return -1;
    }
